@Worker.timer(\"actor/sync_model_to_rollout\")\n    async def sync_model_to_rollout(self) -> None:\n        if self.enable_offload:\n            if not self.is_optimizer_offloaded:\n                self.offload_optimizer()\n\n            if self.is_weight_offloaded:\n                self.load_param_and_grad(self.device, False)\n\n        state_dict = self.get_rollout_state_dict()\n\n        async def send_func(data):\n            if not self._is_weight_sender:\n                return\n            await self.broadcast(\n                data,\n                groups=[\n                    (self._group_name, 0),\n                    (self._rollout_group_name, self._rollout_all_ranks),\n                ],\n                src=(self._group_name, 0),\n                async_op=True,\n                options=self._sync_weight_comm_options,\n            ).async_wait()\n\n        async def recv_func():\n            return await self.recv(\n                src_group_name=self._rollout_group_name,\n                src_rank=0,\n                async_op=True,\n                options=self._sync_weight_comm_options,\n            ).async_wait()\n\n        if not self.weight_syncer.sender_initialized():\n            await self.weight_syncer.init_sender(\n                state_dict=state_dict,\n                send=send_func,\n                recv=recv_func,\n                param_names_need_sync=self.param_names_need_sync,\n                is_sender=self._is_weight_sender,\n            )\n\n        version = (\n            self.get_rollout_sync_version()\n            if hasattr(self, \"get_rollout_sync_version\")\n            else self.version\n        )\n        await self.weight_syncer.sync(state_dict, send_func,     self.offload_param_and_grad(True)\n\n    @Worker.timer(\"actor/recv_traj\")\n    async def recv_rollout_trajectories(self, input_channel: Channel) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Receive rollout recv_list.append(trajectory)\n\n        self.rollout_batch = convert_trajectories_to_batch(recv_list)\n\n        self.rollout_batch = self._process_received_rollout_batch(self.rollout_batch)\n\n    def _process_received_rollout_batch(\n        self, rollout_batch: dict[str, torch.Tensor]\n    ) -> dict[str, torch.Tensor]:\n        \"\"\"\n        original shape: [rollout_epoch x n_chunk_steps, bsz, num_action_chunks, ...]\n        target shape: [n_chunk_steps, rollout_epoch x bsz, num_action_chunks, ...]\n        \"\"\"\n        rollout_epoch = self.cfg.env.train.rollout_epoch\n        rollout_batch = process_nested_dict_for_adv(rollout_batch, rollout_epoch)\n\n        if (\n            not self.cfg.env.train.auto_reset\n            and not self.cfg.env.train.ignore_terminations\n        ):\n            dones = rollout_batch[\n                \"dones\"\n            ]  # [n_chunk_step, rollout_epoch x bsz, num_action_chunks]\n            loss_mask, loss_mask_sum = compute_loss_mask(dones)\n\n            if self.cfg.algorithm.reward_type == \"chunk_level\":\n                loss_mask = loss_mask.any(dim=-1, keepdim=True)\n                loss_mask_sum = loss_mask_sum[..., -1:]\n\n            rollout_batch[\"loss_mask\"] = loss_mask\n            rollout_batch[\"loss_mask_sum\"] = loss_mask_sum\n\n        # filter data by rewards\n        if self.cfg.algorithm.get(\"filter_rewards\", False):\n            rewards = rollout_batch[\n                \"rewards\"\n            ]  # [n_chunk_step, batch, num_action_chunks]\n            if rollout_batch.get(\"loss_mask\", None) is not None:\n                rewards = rewards * rollout_batch[\"loss_mask\"]\n            n_chunk_step, batch_size, num_action_chunks = rewards.shape\n\n            group_size = self.cfg.algorithm.group_size\n            assert batch_size % group_size == 0, (\n                f\"batch {batch_size} not divisible by group_size {group_size}\"\n            )\n            n_prompts = batch_size // group_size\n\n            # calculate rewards by prompt\n            rewards = rewards.transpose(\n                0, 1\n            )  # [batch, n_chunk_step, num_action_chunks]\n            rewards = rewards.reshape(rewards.shape[0], -1)  # [batch, n_step]\n            reward_matrix = rewards.reshape(\n                n_prompts, group_size, rewards.shape[-1]\n            )  # [n_prompts, group_size, n_step]\n            reward_matrix = reward_matrix.sum(dim=-1)  # [n_prompts, group_size]\n            mean_reward_in_group = reward_matrix.mean(dim=1)  # [n_prompts]\n\n            # mask\n            reward_filter_mask = (\n                mean_reward_in_group >= self.cfg.algorithm.rewards_lower_bound\n            ) & (\n                mean_reward_in_group <= self.cfg.algorithm.rewards_upper_bound\n            )  # [n_prompts]\n\n            # extend mask dimension\n            reward_filter_mask = reward_filter_mask.repeat_interleave(\n                group_size\n            )  # [batch]\n            reward_filter_mask = (\n                reward_filter_mask.unsqueeze(0).expand(n_chunk_step, -1).unsqueeze(-1)\n            )  # [n_chunk_step, batch, 1]\n\n            # update loss_mask\n            if rollout_batch.get(\"loss_mask\", None) is not None:\n                rollout_batch[\"loss_mask\"] = (\n                    reward_filter_mask & rollout_batch[\"loss_mask\"]\n                )\n            else:\n                rollout_batch[\"loss_mask\"] = reward_filter_mask\n\n        return rollout_batch\n\n    @Worker.timer(\"actor/compute_adv\")\n    def compute_advantages_and_returns(self) -> dict[str, torch.Tensor]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Compute the advantages and returns.\n        \"\"\"\n        if self.cfg.algorithm.adv_type == \"opd\":\n            self.compute_opd_teacher_logprobs()\n\n        kwargs = {\n            \"task_type\": self.cfg.runner.task_type,\n            \"adv_type\": self.cfg.algorithm.adv_type,\n            \"rewards\": self.rollout_batch[\"rewards\"],\n            \"dones\": self.rollout_batch[\"dones\"],\n            \"values\": self.rollout_batch.get(\"prev_values\", None),\n            \"prev_logprobs\": self.rollout_batch.get(\"prev_logprobs\", None),\n            \"teacher_logprobs\": self.rollout_batch.get(\"teacher_logprobs\", None),\n            \"num_action_chunks\": self.cfg.actor.model.num_action_chunks,\n            \"gamma\": self.rollout_batch.update(advantages_and_returns)\n        if kwargs[\"loss_mask\"] is not None:\n            self.rollout_batch.update({\"loss_mask\": kwargs[\"loss_mask\"]})\n        if kwargs[\"loss_mask_sum\"] is not None:\n            self.rollout_batch.update({\"loss_mask_sum\": kwargs[\"loss_mask_sum\"]})\n\n        rollout_metrics = compute_rollout_metrics(self.rollout_batch)\n        return rollout_metrics\n\n    @Worker.timer(\"actor/compute_opd_teacher_logprobs\")\n    def compute_opd_teacher_logprobs(self) -> None:\n        assert self.rollout_batch.get(\"teacher_logprobs\", None) is None, (\n            \"OPD teacher_logprobs must be computed after rollout on actor workers.\"\n        )\n        assert self.cfg.rollout.get(\"expert_model\", None) is not None, (\n            \"OPD requires rollout.expert_model as teacher model config.\"\n        )\n        assert \"forward_inputs\" in self.rollout_batch, (\n            \"OPD teacher logprob computation requires rollout forward_inputs.\"\n        )\n        assert \"prev_logprobs\" in self.rollout_batch, (\n            \"OPD teacher logprob computation requires student prev_logprobs.\"\n        )\n        assert SupportedModel(self.cfg.actor.model.model_type) in [\n            SupportedModel.OPENVLA,\n            SupportedModel.OPENVLA_OFT,\n        ], \"OPD teacher logprob computation currently supports OpenVLA models.\"\n\n        prev_logprobs = self.rollout_batch[\"prev_logprobs\"]\n        time_dim, batch_dim = prev_logprobs.shape[:2]\n        flat_batch_size = time_dim * batch_dim\n\n        assert self.enable_offload and self.is_weight_offloaded, (\n            \"OPD teacher logprob computation expects actor weights to be \"\n            \"offloaded before moving the teacher model to GPU.\"\n        )\n        teacher_model = self._get_opd_teacher_model()\n        teacher_model.to(self.device)\n\n        flat_forward_inputs = flatten_nested_tensor_time_batch(\n            self.rollout_batch[\"forward_inputs\"], (\"forward_inputs\",)\n        )\n        num_chunks = (\n            flat_batch_size + self.cfg.actor.micro_batch_size - 1\n        ) // self.cfg.actor.micro_batch_size\n        teacher_logprobs = []\n        kwargs = {\n            \"temperature\": self.cfg.rollout.sampling_params.temperature_train,\n            \"top_k\": self.cfg.rollout.sampling_params.top_k,\n        }\n        with torch.no_grad():\n            for micro_batch in split_dict_to_chunk(flat_forward_inputs, num_chunks):\n                micro_batch = put_tensor_device(micro_batch, self.device)\n                with self.amp_context:\n                    teacher_output = teacher_model(\n                        forward_inputs=micro_batch,\n                        compute_logprobs=True,\n                        compute_entropy=False,\n                        compute_values=False,\n                        use_cache=False,\n                        **kwargs,\n                    )\n                teacher_logprobs.append(teacher_output[\"logprobs\"].detach().cpu())\n\n        teacher_logprobs = torch.cat(teacher_logprobs, dim=0)\n        expected_shape = (flat_batch_size, *prev_logprobs.shape[2:])\n        assert teacher_logprobs.shape == expected_shape, (\n            f\"teacher_logprobs shape {teacher_logprobs.shape} must match \"\n            f\"flattened student        teacher_model.to(\"cpu\")\n        clear_memory()\n\n    def _get_opd_teacher_model(self):\n        if self._opd_teacher_model is None:\n            teacher_model_config = build_expert_model_config(\n                self.cfg, self.cfg.actor.model\n            )\n            teacher_model = get_model(teacher_model_config)\n            if self.cfg.runner.get(\"expert_ckpt_path\", None):\n                teacher_model_dict = torch.load(\n                    self.cfg.runner.expert_ckpt_path, map_location=\"cpu\"\n                )\n                teacher_model.load_state_dict(teacher_model_dict)\n            teacher_model.eval()\n            teacher_model.requires_grad_(False)\n            teacher_model.to(\"cpu\")\n            self._opd_teacher_model = teacher_model\n        return self._opd_teacher_model\n\n    def _build_sft_data_loader(self):\n        if SupportedModel(self.cfg.actor.model.model_type) in [SupportedModel.OPENPI]:\n            repo_id = resolve_lerobot_repo_id(self.cfg.actor.get(\"sft_data_path\"))\n            if repo_id is None:\n                raise ValueError(\n                    \"actor.sft_data_path must be set to a local dataset path or \"\n                    \"LeRobot repo id when enable_sft_co_train=True.\"\n                )\n\n            import openpi.training.data_loader as _data\n\n            from rlinf.models.embodiment.openpi.dataconfig import get_openpi_config\n\n            if \"config_name\" not in self.cfg.actor:\n                raise ValueError(\n                    \"config_name is required when enable_sft_co_train=True\"\n                )\n            training_config_name = self.cfg.actor.config_name\n            data_loader_config = get_openpi_config(\n                training_config_name,\n                model_path=self.cfg.actor.model.model_path,\n                repo_id=repo_id,\n                data_kwargs=getattr(self.cfg.actor.model, \"openpi_data\", None),\n            )\n            self.data_loader = _data.create_data_loader(\n                data_loader_config, framework=\"pytorch\",        else:\n            raise KeyError(\n                f\"not support such model type {self.cfg.actor.model.model_type} for SFT right now.\"\n            )\n\n    def _train_sft_epoch(\n        self, metrics_data: dict[str, torch.Tensor], loss: torch.Tensor\n    ) -> torch.Tensor:\n        \"\"\"\n        Train one epoch of SFT.\n        \"\"\"\n        metrics_data[\"ppo_loss\"] = loss.clone().detach().item()\n\n        # Get next data batch\n        try:\n            observation, actions = next(self.sft_iterator)\n        except StopIteration:\n            self.train_epoch += 1\n            self.data_loader.set_epoch(self.train_epoch)\n            self.sft_iterator = iter(self.data_loader)\n            observation, actions = next(self.sft_iterator)\n\n        sft_loss = self.model(\n            data=(observation, actions),\n            forward_type=ForwardType.SFT,\n        )\n        metrics_data[\"sft_loss\"] = sft_loss.detach().item()\n        total_loss = loss + self.sft_loss_weight * sft_loss\n        loss = total_loss\n\n        metrics_data[\"loss_ratio\"] = (\n            np.abs(metrics_data[\"sft_loss\"]) / np.abs(metrics_data[\"ppo_loss\"])\n            if np.abs(metrics_data[\"ppo_loss\"]) > 0\n            else float(\"inf\")\n        )\n        if metrics_data[\"loss_ratio\"] > 1e5:\n            self.logger.warning(\n                \"SFT/PPO loss imbalance detected: \"\n                f\"ratio={metrics_data['loss_ratio']:.3e}, \"\n                f\"sft_loss={metrics_data['sft_loss']:.6f}, \"\n                f\"ppo_loss={metrics_data['ppo_loss']:.6f}, \"\n                f\"sft_loss_weight={self.sft_loss_weight:.6f}\"\n            )\n        return loss\n\n    def _prepare_dvac_train_step(self) -> None:\n        forward_inputs = self.rollout_batch.get(\"forward_inputs\")\n        if not isinstance(forward_inputs, dict):\n            raise ValueError(\"DVAC gradient weighting requires forward_inputs.\")\n        variance_key = f\"dvac_v_l{self.dvac_selected_l}\"\n        if variance_key not in forward_inputs:\n            raise ValueError(f\"Missing train-time DVAC tensor: {variance_key}\")\n        variance = forward_inputs.pop(variance_key)\n\n        local_stats = local_log_v_sufficient_statistics(\n            variance, log_eps=self.dvac_recent_stats.log_eps\n        ).to(self.device)\n        torch.distributed.all_reduce(local_stats, op=torch.distributed.ReduceOp.SUM)\n        count, value_sum, value_sq_sum = local_stats.detach().cpu().tolist()\n        current_stats = DVACStepStats(\n            runner_step=int(self.version),\n            count=int(round(count)),\n            value_sum=float(value_sum),\n            value_sq_sum=float(value_sq_sum),\n        )\n\n        weights, clipped_z, warmup, history = self.dvac_recent_stats.compute_weights(\n            variance\n        )\n        forward_inputs[\"dvac_weights\"] = weights\n        self._dvac_pending_step = {\n            \"runner_step\": int(self.version),\n            \"current_stats\": current_stats,\n            \"history\": history,\n            \"warmup\": warmup,\n            \"variance\": variance.detach().cpu().contiguous(),\n            \"weights\": weights.detach().cpu().contiguous(),\n            \"clipped_z\": clipped_z.detach().cpu().contiguous(),\n            \"advantages\": self.rollout_batch[\"advantages\"].detach().cpu().contiguous(),\n            \"rewards\": self.rollout_batch[\"rewards\"].detach().cpu().contiguous(),\n            \"loss_mask\": (\n                None\n                if self.rollout_batch.get(\"loss_mask\") is None\n                else self.rollout_batch[\"loss_mask\"].detach().cpu().contiguous()\n            ),\n            \"metrics\": {\n                \"actor/dvac_warmup\": float(warmup),\n                \"actor/dvac_history_mean\": float(history[\"history_mean\"]),\n                \"actor/dvac_history_std\": float(history[\"history_std\"]),\n                \"actor/dvac_current_mean\": current_stats.mean,\n                \"actor/dvac_current_std\": current_stats.std,\n                \"actor/dvac_weight_mean\": float(weights.float().mean().item()),\n                \"actor/dvac_weight_sq_mean\": float(\n                    weights.float().square().mean().item()\n                ),\n                \"actor/dvac_z_low_clip_fraction\": float(\n                    (clipped_z <= -self.dvac_recent_stats.z_clip)\n                    .float()\n                    .mean()\n                    .item()\n                ),\n                \"actor/dvac_z_high_clip_fraction\": float(\n                    (clipped_z >= self.dvac_recent_stats.z_clip)\n                    .float()\n                    .mean()\n                    .item()\n                ),\n            },\n        }\n\n    def _write_dvac_step_artifact(self, pending: dict) -> None:\n        if not bool(self.dvac_train_cfg.get(\"save_step_tensors\", True)):\n            return\n        path = self.dvac_output_dir / (\n            f\"runner_step_{int(pending['runner_step']):04d}.pt\"\n        )\n        if path.exists():\n            raise FileExistsError(f\"DVAC step artifact already exists: {path}\")\n        temporary = path.with_suffix(\".partial.pt\")\n        torch.save(\n            {\n                \"schema_version\": 1,\n                \"runner_step\":     \"loss_mask\": pending[\"loss_mask\"],\n            },\n            temporary,\n        )\n        os.replace(temporary, path)\n\n    @Worker.timer(\"run_training\")\n    def run_training(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Run the training process using the received rollout batch.\n        \"\"\"\n        if self.is_weight_offloaded:\n            self.load_param_and_grad(self.device)\n        if self.is_optimizer_offloaded:\n            self.load_optimizer(self.device)\n\n        if self.cfg.algorithm.loss_type == \"opd\":\n            target_steps = \"loss_mask_sum\",\n            ]:\n                assert key in self.rollout_batch, f\"OPD training requires {key}.\"\n                self.rollout_batch[key] = trim_nested_tensor_time_dim(\n                    self.rollout_batch[key], target_steps, (key,)\n                )\n\n        self.model.train()\n        if self.dvac_train_enabled:\n            self._prepare_dvac_train_step()\n g.manual_seed(self.cfg.actor.seed + self._rank)\n        shuffle_id = torch.randperm(rollout_size, generator=g)\n\n        with torch.no_grad():\n            self.rollout_batch = process_nested_dict_for_train(\n                self.rollout_batch, shuffle_id\n            )\n\n        # Split to make minibatch iterator for updating the actor\n        # See PPO paper for details. https://arxiv.org/abs/1707.06347\n        rollout_size = self.rollout_batch[\"prev_logprobs\"].size(0)\n        batch_size_per_rank = self.cfg.actor.global_batch_size // self._world_size\n        assert rollout_size % batch_size_per_rank == 0, (\n            f\"{rollout_size} is not divisible by {batch_size_per_rank}\"\n        )\n        metrics = {}\n        update_epoch = self.cfg.algorithm.get(\"update_epoch\", 1)\n        for _ in range(update_epoch):\n            rollout_dataloader_iter = split_dict_to_chunk(\n                self.rollout_batch,\n                rollout_size // batch_size_per_rank,\n            )\n            for train_global_batch in rollout_dataloader_iter:\n                # split batch into micro_batches\n                train_global_batch_size = train_global_batch[\"prev_logprobs\"].shape[0]\n                assert (\n                    train_global_batch_size\n                    == self.cfg.actor.global_batch_size\n                    // torch.distributed.get_world_size()\n                )\n                assert train_global_batch_size % self.cfg.actor.micro_batch_size == 0, train_global_batch,\n                    train_global_batch_size // self.cfg.actor.micro_batch_size,\n                )\n\n                self.optimizer.zero_grad()\n                for idx, batch in enumerate(train_micro_batch):\n                    self.train_micro_batch(\n                        micro_batch=batch,\n                        metrics=metrics,\n                        is_last=(idx + 1) == self.gradient_accumulation,\n                    )\n                    # avoid gpu memory leak\n                    train_micro_batch[idx] = None\n                    del batch\n\n                self.torch_platform.empty_cache()\n\n                grad_norm, lr_list = self.optimizer_step()\n                data = {\n                    \"actor/grad_norm\": grad_norm,\n                    \"actor/lr\": lr_list[0],\n                }\n                if len(lr_list) > 1:\n                    data[\"critic/lr\"] = lr_list[1]\n                append_to_dict(metrics, data)\n        if self.dvac_train_enabled:\n            append_to_dict(metrics, self._dvac_pending_step[\"metrics\"])\n\n        # put LR scheduler step here\n        self.lr_scheduler.step()\n        self.optimizer.zero_grad()\n        clear_memory()\n        explained_variance_stats = pop_critic_explained_variance_stats(metrics)\n        mean_metric_dict = {key: np.mean(value) for key, value in metrics.items()}\n        mean_metric_dict = all_reduce_dict(\n            mean_metric_dict, op=torch.distributed.ReduceOp.AVG\n        )\n        if self.dvac_train_enabled:\n            weight_mean            self._write_dvac_step_artifact(pending)\n            self.dvac_recent_stats.push(pending[\"current_stats\"])\n            self._dvac_pending_step = None\n        if mean_metric_dict[CRITIC_EXPLAINED_VARIANCE_KEY] = (\n                compute_critic_explained_variance_from_stats(reduced_stats).item()\n            )\n\n        return mean_metric_dict\n\n    def train_micro_batch(\n        self,\n        micro_batch: dict[str, torch.Tensor],\n        metrics: dict[str, list[float]],\n        *,\n        is_last: bool,\n    ) -> None:\n        micro_batch =         for key, value in forward_inputs.items()\n                if key != \"dvac_weights\"\n            }\n\n        kwargs = {}\n        if SupportedModel(self.cfg.actor.model.model_type) in [\n            SupportedModel.OPENVLA,\n            SupportedModel.OPENVLA_OFT,\n        ]:\n            kwargs[\"temperature\"] = self.cfg.rollout.sampling_params.temperature_train\n            kwargs[\"top_k\"] = self.cfg.rollout.sampling_params.top_k\n        elif SupportedModel(self.cfg.actor.model.model_type) in [\n            SupportedModel.GR00T,\n            SupportedModel.GR00T_N1D6,\n            SupportedModel.GR00T_N1D7,\n            SupportedModel.ABOT_M0,\n        ]:\n            kwargs[\"prev_logprobs\"] = prev_logprobs\n\n        compute_values = **kwargs,\n            )\n\n        if SupportedModel(self.cfg.actor.model.model_type) in [\n            SupportedModel.GR00T,\n            SupportedModel.GR00T_N1D6,\n           self.cfg.algorithm.clip_log_ratio_max\n                )\n\n        loss, metrics_data = policy_loss(**loss_kwargs)\n        entropy_loss = torch.tensor(0.0, save_checkpoint(self, save_path: str, step: int = 0) -> None:\n        super().save_checkpoint(save_path, step)\n        if not self.dvac_train_enabled:\n            return\n        if  os.replace(temporary, path)\n\n    def load_checkpoint(self, load_path: str) -> None:\n        restored_stats = None\n        if self.dvac_train_enabled:\n            path =     )\n            payload = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n            expected = {\n                \"schema_version\": 1,\n                \"actor_rank\": int(self._rank),\n                \"actor_world_size\": int(self._world_size),\n                \"mode\": self.dvac_train_mode,\n                \"selected_l\": int(self.dvac_selected_l),\n            }\n            for key,       tag: str | None = None,\n        batch_size: int | None = None,\n        merge_fn: Optional[Callable[[list[Any]], Any]] = None,\n        infer_batch_size_fn: Optional[Callable[[Any], int]] = None,\n        timeout_time: float = 0.02,\n        recv_queue_size: int = 0,\n    ):\n        \"\"\"Receive routed batch shards and record their return routes.\n\n        This method is used in env-decoupled mode. It builds a receive plan for the\n        source worker group, receives shard messages from ``channel`` one by one, and\n        stops when all planned items are received or ``timeout_time`` is reached.\n\n        Each received channel item must be a dict with ``batch_index`` (route\n        metadata) and ``batch`` (payload shard).\n\n        The ``batch_index`` values are stored in ``self.batch_router[tag]`` so a\n        later send call can split the response and send each shard back to the original\n        source rank. The received payload shards are validated, then merged with\n        ``merge_fn`` or the default ``merge_batches``.\n\n        Args:\n            group_name: Source worker group name.\n            channel: Channel used to receive routed batch shards.\n            route_key: Optional key used to separate independent routed streams.\n            tag: Routing tag used to build receive keys and index recorded routes.\n            batch_size: Expected batch size for each planned receive entry.\n            merge_fn: Optional custom function for merging received shards.\n            infer_batch_size_fn: Optional function used to infer shard batch size during\n                validation.\n            timeout_time: Maximum time in seconds to wait before finalizing partial\n                results.\n            recv_queue_size: Number of receive queue entries used when building the\n                receive plan.\n\n        Returns:\n\n            A merged payload and its split sizes. If only one shard is received, the\n            current implementation returns that shard directly.\n        \"\"\"\n        from rlinf.scheduler import (\n            decoupled_build_recv_plan,\n            get_batch_size,\n            get_group_world_size,\n            merge_batches,\n        )\n\n        world_size = get_group_world_size(self._manager_proxy, group_name)\n        plan = decoupled_build_recv_plan(\n            src_group_name=group_name,\n            dst_group_name=self.worker_address.root_group_name,\n            recv_rank=None,\n            src_world_size=self._world_size,\n            dst_world_size=world_size,\n            tag=tag,\n            route_key=route_key,\n            batch_size=batch_size,\n            recv_queue_size=recv_queue_size,\n        )\n\n        def _finalize(received_items: list[Any]):\n            if not received_items:\n                assert False, \"received_items is empty\"\n\n            # get the tag from the received_items\n            _, _, _, tag = split_channel_message(received_items[0][\"batch_index\"])\n\n            assert tag in self.batch_router, (\n                f\"{tag=} need to be already in the batch_router\"\n            )\n            # Save the batch_index to the batch_router.\n            list_received_items = []\n            for item in received_items:\n                batch_index = item[\"batch_index\"]\n                received_item = item[\"batch\"]\n                list_received_items.append(received_item)\n                # Save the batch_index to the batch_router.\n                self.batch_router[tag].append(batch_index)\n            received_items = list_received_items\n            split_sizes = [\n                get_batch_size(item, infer_batch_size_fn) for item in received_items\n            ]\n\n            if merge_fn is not None:\n                return merge_fn(received_items), split_sizes\n            if len(received_items) == 1:\n                return received_items[0]\n            return merge_batches(received_items), split_sizes\n\n        timeout_time = timeout_time + time.time()\n        get_items = None\n        max_item_num = len(plan.entries)\n        get_item_num = 0\n        received_items = []\n        while get_item_num < max_item_num:\n            # get the items\n            if get_items is None:\n                get_items = channel.get(\n                    key=plan.entries[get_item_num].key, async_op=True\n                )\n            else:\n                # Now, the worker is getting a item, sleep to wait\n                await asyncio.sleep(0.0001)\n\n            # handle the get_items finish\n            if get_items.done():\n                # save the data and init the get_items to get next data\n                received_items.append(await get_items.async_wait())\n                get_items = None\n                get_item_num = get_item_num + 1\n\n            # handle the timeout case\n            if time.time() >= timeout_time:\n                max_item_num = get_item_num\n                if get_items is not None:\n                    received_items.append(await get_items.async_wait())\n                    get_items = None\n                    get_item_num = get_item_num + 1\n\n        return _finalize(received_items)\n\n    def send_to_recorded_batch_routes(\n        self,\n        group_name: str,\n        channel: Any | None,\n        data: Any,\n        *,\n        route_key: Any = None,\n        tag: str | None = None,\n        split_fn: Optional[Callable[[Any, list[int]], list[Any]]] = None,\n        split_sizes: list[int],\n    ):\n        \"\"\"Send split batch results back using recorded batch routes.\n\n        This method is used after a previous receive call has populated\n        ``self.batch_router[tag]`` with batch indices from incoming messages.\n        The outgoing ``data`` is split according to ``split_sizes`` and each shard is\n        sent to the rank encoded in the corresponding recorded batch index.\n\n        Each outgoing channel item is a dict with ``batch_index`` (recorded batch\n        index) and ``batch`` (payload shard).\n\n        After all shards are queued, the recorded batch indices for ``tag`` are cleared\n        to avoid reusing stale routes.\n\n        Args:\n            group_name: Destination worker group name.\n            channel: Channel used to send the split payloads.\n            data: Payload to split and send.\n            route_key: Optional key used to separate independent routed streams.\n            tag: Routing tag whose recorded batch indices should be consumed.\n            split_fn: Optional custom splitter. If omitted, ``split_batch`` is used.\n            split_sizes: Batch sizes used to split ``data``. Must have the same length\n                as ``self.batch_router[tag]``.\n\n        Returns:\n\n            AsyncRouteWork wrapping the async channel put operations.\n        \"\"\"\n        from rlinf.scheduler import build_send_key, split_batch\n        from rlinf.scheduler.collective import AsyncRouteWork\n\n        assert tag in self.batch_router, (\n            f\"{tag=} need to be already in the batch_router\"\n        )\n\n        assert len(self.batch_router[tag]) > 0, f\"{self.batch_router[tag]=} is empty\"\n        assert len(self.batch_router[tag]) == len(split_sizes), (\n            f\"{self.batch_router[tag]=} length should equal {split_sizes=} length\"\n        )\n\n        payloads = (\n            split_fn(data, split_sizes)\n            if split_fn is not None\n            else split_batch(data, split_sizes)\n        )\n\n        works = []\n        for i, payload in enumerate(payloads):\n            batch_index = self.batch_router[tag][i]\n            send_rank, _, mode, _ = split_channel_message(batch_index)\n            # After enabling env_decoupled_mode, the data sending format is as follows:\n            # {\n            #     \"batch_index\": batch_index,\n            #     \"batch\": batch,\n            # }\n            # The batch_index is the index of the batch in the data.\n            # The batch is the data to send.\n            # batch_index: {send_rank}_{batch_idx}_{mode}_{tag}\n            # The send_rank is the rank of the worker that originally sent the data.\n            # The batch_idx is the index of the batch in the data.\n            # The tag is the tag of the data.\n            senditem = {\n                \"batch_index\": batch_index,\n                \"batch\": payload,\n            }\n            key = build_send_key(\n                src_group_name=self.worker_address.root_group_name,\n                dst_group_name=group_name,\n                src_rank=None,\n                dst_rank=send_rank,\n                tag=tag if mode is None else f\"{mode}_{tag}\",\n                route_key=route_key,\n            )\n            work = channel.put(\n                item=senditem,\n                key=key,\n                async_op=True,\n            )\n            works.append(work)\n\n        # clear the batch_router for the next send\n        self.batch_router[tag] = []\n        return AsyncRouteWork(works, lambda _: None)\n\n    def update_dagger_beta(self):\n        if self.expert_model is None or not self.enable_dagger:\n            return\n\n        if self._dagger_sampling_params[\"beta_schedule\"] == \"exponential\":\n            self._dagger_sampling_params[\"beta\"] = max(\n                self._dagger_sampling_params[\"beta_min\"],\n                self._dagger_sampling_params[\"beta\"]\n                * self._dagger_sampling_params[\"beta_decay\"],\n            )\n        else:\n            raise NotImplementedError(\n                f\"Beta schedule {self._dagger_sampling_params['beta_schedule']} is not implemented\"\n            )\n\n    @Worker.timer(\"predict\")\n    def predict(\n        self, env_obs: dict[str, Any], mode: Literal[\"train\", \"eval\"] = \"train\"\n    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, dict[str, Any]]:\n        kwargs = (\n            self._train_sampling_params\n            if mode == \"train\"\n            else self._eval_sampling_params\n        )\n\n        if SupportedModel(self.model_cfg.model_type) in [\n            SupportedModel.OPENPI,\n            SupportedModel.OPENPI_RLINF,\n            SupportedModel.EVO1,\n            SupportedModel.MLP_POLICY,\n            SupportedModel.GR00T,\n            SupportedModel.GR00T_N1D6,\n            SupportedModel.GR00T_N1D7,\n            SupportedModel.ABOT_M0,\n            SupportedModel.DREAMZERO,\n            SupportedModel.CNN_POLICY,\n            SupportedModel.CFG_MODEL,\n            SupportedModel.MOLMOACT2,\n        ]:\n            if self.enable_dagger:\n                kwargs = {\"mode\": \"eval\"}\n            else:\n                kwargs = {\"mode\": mode}\n\n        if SupportedModel(self.model_cfg.model_type) in [\n            SupportedModel.CNN_POLICY,\n            SupportedModel.FLOW_POLICY,\n            SupportedModel.MLP_POLICY,\n        ]:\n            kwargs[\"return_obs\"] = not hasattr(self.hf_model, \"q_head\")\n\n        if (\n            self.dvac_telemetry_enabled\n            and mode == \"eval\"\n            and SupportedModel(self.model_cfg.model_type) == SupportedModel.OPENPI\n        ):\n            kwargs[\"return_dvac_telemetry\"] = True\n        if self.dvac_train_enabled and mode == \"train\":\n            kwargs[\"return_dvac_telemetry\"] = True\n\n        only_save_expert = self.algorithm_cfg.get(\"dagger\", {}).get(\n            \"only_save_expert\", True\n        )\n\n        if mode == \"train\" and self.expert_model is not None and self.enable_dagger:\n            # training with expert model. Beta-probability acting.\n            use_expert = torch.rand(1).item() < self._dagger_sampling_params[\"beta\"]\n        else:\n            use_expert = False\n\n        with torch.no_grad():\n            expert_label_flag = False\n            # Decide which model to act via use_expert\n            if use_expert:\n                actions, result = self.expert_model.predict_action_batch(\n                    env_obs=env_obs,\n                    **kwargs,\n                )\n                expert_label_flag = True\n            else:\n                actions, result = self.hf_model.predict_action_batch(\n                    env_obs=env_obs,\n                    **kwargs,\n                )\n\n            # Decide re-label or not\n            if (\n                not only_save_expert  # only re-label in classic dagger mode\n                and not use_expert  # only re-label if not using expert\n                and self.expert_model is not None  # only re-label if expert exists\n                and self.enable_dagger  # only re-label in DAgger mode\n                and mode == \"train\"  # only re-label in train mode\n            ):\n                _, expert_result = self.expert_model.predict_action_batch(\n                    env_obs=env_obs,\n                    **kwargs,\n                )\n                expert_forward_inputs = expert_result[\"forward_inputs\"]\n                expert_target = expert_forward_inputs[\"model_action\"]\n                expert_action = expert_forward_inputs[\"action\"]\n                if expert_target is not None:\n                    result[\"forward_inputs\"][\"action\"] = expert_action\n                    result[\"forward_inputs\"][\"model_action\"] = expert_target\n                expert_label_flag = True\n\n        if self.dvac_train_enabled and mode == \"train\":\n            telemetry = result.pop(\"dvac_telemetry\", None)\n            if telemetry is None or \"z_endpoint\" not in telemetry:\n                raise ValueError(\"OpenPI did not return DVAC endpoint telemetry.\")\n            variance = compute_endpoint_variance(\n                telemetry[\"z_endpoint\"], self.dvac_selected_l\n            )\n            result[\"forward_inputs\"][f\"dvac_v_l{self.dvac_selected_l}\"] = (\n                variance.detach().cpu().contiguous()\n            )\n\n        if isinstance(actions, np.ndarray):\n            actions = torch.from_numpy(actions)\n\n        result[\"expert_label_flag\"] = bool(expert_label_flag)\n        return actions, result\n\n    def _predict_rollout_actions(\n        self,\n        env_obs: dict[str, Any],\n        mode: Literal[\"train\", \"eval\"] = \"train\",\n        final_obs: dict[str, Any] | None = None,\n        rlt_switch_flags: torch.Tensor | None = None,\n        intervene_requested: torch.Tensor | None = None,\n    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, dict[str, Any]]:\n        if self.rlt_feature_model is not None:\n            return predict_rlt_actions(\n                policy_model=self.hf_model,\n                feature_model=self.rlt_feature_model,\n                rlt_route=self.rlt_route,\n                env_obs=env_obs,\n                final_obs=final_obs,\n                mode=mode,\n                version=self.version,\n                rlt_switch_flags=rlt_switch_flags,\n                intervene_requested=intervene_requested,\n                expert_model=self.expert_model,\n            )\n        return self.predict(env_obs, mode=mode)\n\n    def _build_policy_output(\n        self,\n        actions: torch.Tensor,\n        result: dict[str, Any],\n        *,\n        final_obs: dict[str, Any] | None = None,\n    ) -> PolicyOutput:\n        intervene_flags = result.get(\"intervene_flags\")\n        if (\n            intervene_flags is None\n            and self.enable_dagger\n            and result.get(\"expert_label_flag\", False)\n        ):\n            intervene_flags = torch.full(\n                (actions.shape[0], self.model_cfg.num_action_chunks),\n                True,\n                dtype=torch.bool,\n                device=actions.device,\n            )\n        return PolicyOutput(\n            actions=actions,\n            prev_logprobs=result[\"prev_logprobs\"] if self.collect_prev_infos else None,\n            prev_values=result[\"prev_values\"] if self.collect_prev_infos else None,\n            bootstrap_values=self.get_bootstrap_values(final_obs),\n            intervene_flags=intervene_flags,\n            forward_inputs=result[\"forward_inputs\"],\n            versions=torch.full_like(\n                result[\"prev_logprobs\"],\n                float(self.version),\n                dtype=torch.float32,\n            ),\n        )\n\n    def get_bootstrap_values(\n        self, final_obs: dict[str, Any] | None\n    ) -> torch.Tensor | None:\n        if final_obs is None:\n            return None\n        if not (\n            hasattr(self.hf_model, \"value_head\") or hasattr(self.hf_model, \"q_head\")\n        ):\n            return None\n        with torch.no_grad():\n            actions, result = self._predict_rollout_actions(final_obs)\n            if \"prev_values\" in result and result[\"prev_values\"] is not None:\n                final_values = result[\"prev_values\"]\n            else:\n                final_values = torch.zeros_like(actions[:, :1], dtype=torch.float32)\n        return final_values[:, :1].cpu().contiguous()\n\n    @Worker.timer(\"sync_model_from_actor\")\n    async def sync_model_from_actor(self):\n        \"\"\"Sync model parameters from the actor worker.\"\"\"\n\n        async def recv_func() -> Any:\n            return await self.broadcast(\n                None,\n                groups=[\n                    (self.actor_group_name, self.actor_weight_src_rank),\n                    (self._group_name, self._weight_sync_rollout_ranks),\n                ],\n                src=(self.actor_group_name, self.actor_weight_src_rank),\n                async_op=True,\n                options=self._sync_weight_comm_options,\n            ).async_wait()\n\n        async def send_func(data: Any) -> None:\n            if not self._weight_sync_is_sender:\n                return\n            actor_world_size = self.placement.get_world_size(\"actor\")\n            for actor_rank in range(actor_world_size):\n                await self.send(\n                    data,\n                    dst_group_name=self.actor_group_name,\n                    dst_rank=actor_rank,\n                    async_op=True,\n                    options=self._sync_weight_comm_options,\n                ).async_wait()\n\n        if not self.weight_syncer.receiver_initialized():\n            await self.weight_syncer.init_receiver(\n                state_dict=self.hf_model.state_dict(),\n                recv=recv_func,\n                send=send_func,\n            )\n\n        applied_version = await self.weight_syncer.apply(self.hf_model, recv_func)\n        self.version = applied_version\n        if self.finished_episodes is None:\n            self.finished_episodes = (\n                self.version * self.total_num_train_envs * self.rollout_epoch\n            )\n        if hasattr(self.hf_model, \"set_global_step\"):\n            self.hf_model.set_global_step(applied_version)\n\n        gc.collect()\n        self.torch_platform.empty_cache()\n\n    @Worker.timer(\"generate_one_epoch\")\n    async def generate_one_epoch(self, input_channel: Channel, output_channel: Channel):\n        self.update_dagger_beta()\n        for _ in range(self.n_train_chunk_steps):\n            for stage_id in range(self.num_pipeline_stages):\n                env_output = await self.recv_from(\n _merge_optional_flag_tensors(\n        self,\n        obs_dicts: list[dict[str, Any]],\n        flags_list: list[torch.Tensor | None],\n    ) -> torch.Tensor | None:\n        if not any(flags is not SOURCE_JSON {"path": "/data/chenyiteng/projects/rlinf-shenzhen/worktrees/pi0-dvac-grpo-current/rlinf/algorithms/utils.py", "sha256": "20dca7b791beadcb9ad66c790d1e5f92f7ac4c5758c5588393797c089784a5a8", "text": "# Copyright 2025 The RLinf Authors.\n#\n# Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n# you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n# You may obtain a copy of the License at\n#\n#     https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n#\n# Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n# distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n# WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n# See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n# limitations under the License.\n\nfrom typing import Optional\n\nimport torch\n\n\ndef huber_loss(error: torch.Tensor, delta: float) -> torch.Tensor:\n    return torch.where(\n        error.abs() < delta, 0.5 * error**2, delta * (error.abs() - 0.5 * delta)\n    )\n\n\ndef kl_penalty(\n    logprob: torch.FloatTensor, ref_logprob: torch.FloatTensor, kl_penalty\n) -> torch.FloatTensor:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute KL divergence given logprob and ref_logprob.\n    Copied from https://github.com/huggingface/trl/blob/main/trl/trainer/ppo_trainer.py#L1104\n    See more description in http://joschu.net/blog/kl-approx.html\n\n    Args:\n        logprob:\n        ref_logprob:\n\n    Returns:\n\n    \"\"\"\n    if kl_penalty in (\"kl\", \"k1\"):\n        return logprob - ref_logprob\n\n    if kl_penalty == \"abs\":\n        return (logprob - ref_logprob).abs()\n\n    if kl_penalty in (\"mse\", \"k2\"):\n        return 0.5 * (logprob - ref_logprob).square()\n\n    # J. Schulman. Approximating kl divergence, 2020.\n    # # URL http://joschu.net/blog/kl-approx.html.\n    if kl_penalty in (\"low_var_kl\", \"k3\"):\n        kl = ref_logprob - logprob\n        # For numerical stability\n        kl = torch.clamp(kl, min=-20, max=20)\n        ratio = torch.exp(kl)\n        kld = (ratio - kl - 1).contiguous()\n        return torch.clamp(kld, min=-10, max=10)\n\n    if kl_penalty == \"full\":\n        # so, here logprob and ref_logprob should contain the logits for every token in vocabulary\n        raise NotImplementedError\n\n    raise NotImplementedError\n\n\ndef preprocess_embodied_advantages_inputs(\n    rewards: torch.Tensor,\n    dones: torch.Tensor,\n    values: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,\n    loss_mask: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,\n    loss_mask_sum: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,\n    **kwargs,\n) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Preprocess inputs before computing advantages & returns.\n    Unify names & formats, align with math interfaces.\n    \"\"\"\n    if kwargs[\"reward_type\"] == \"chunk_level\":\n        # TODO: need check\n        # rewards, dones, loss_mask, loss_mask_sum: [n_chunk_steps, bsz, num_action_chunks] -> [n_chunk_steps, bsz, 1]\n        rewards = rewards.sum(dim=-1, keepdim=True)\n        dones = dones.max(dim=-1, keepdim=True)[0]\n        if loss_mask is not None:\n            loss_mask = loss_mask.max(dim=-1, keepdim=True)[0]\n        if loss_mask_sum is not None:\n            loss_mask_sum = loss_mask_sum.max(dim=-1, keepdim=True)[0]\n\n    num_chunk, bsz, chunk_size =          \"n_steps\": n_steps,\n        }\n    )\n\n    # Transpose(1, 2) -> [num-chunk, chunk-size, bsz]\n    # Reshape -> [n_steps, bsz]\n    # Rewards [n_steps, bsz]\n    rewards = rewards.transpose(1, 2).reshape(n_steps, bsz)\n\n    # Loss Mask (T steps) [bsz, n_steps]\n    if loss_mask is not None:\n        loss_mask = loss_mask.transpose(1, 2).reshape(n_steps, bsz)\n\n    #   )\n        values = flattened_values_full[: n_steps + 1]\n\n    kwargs.update(\n        {\n            \"rewards\": rewards,\n            \"dones\": dones,\n            \"values\": values,\n            \"loss_mask\": loss_mask,\n            \"loss_mask_sum\": loss_mask_sum,\n        }\n    )\n\n    return kwargs\n\n\ndef calculate_scores(\n    rewards: torch.Tensor,\n    dones: torch.Tensor,\n    **kwargs,\n) -> dict:\n    scores = torch.zeros(kwargs[\"batch_size\"])\n    for step in reversed(range(kwargs[\"n_steps\"])):\n        scores = scores * ~dones[step + 1]\n        scores += rewards[step]\n    scores = scores.reshape(-1, kwargs[\"group_size\"])\n\n    kwargs.update(\n        {\n            \"rewards\": scores,\n            \"dones\": dones,\n        }\n    )\n\n    return kwargs\n\n\ndef postprocess_embodied_advantages_outputs(\n    advantages: torch.Tensor,\n    num_chunk: int,\n    chunk_size: int,\n    returns: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,\n    **kwargs,\n) -> loss_mask.shape\n    loss_mask = loss_mask.transpose(0, 1)  # [seq_len, bsz]\n\n    assert rewards.ndim == 1, f\"Unsupported reward shape {rewards.shape}\"\n\n    if kwargs[\"adv_type\"] == \"gae\":\n kwargs.update(\n            {\n                \"rewards\": grouped_rewards,\n            }\n        )\n\n    elif kwargs[\"adv_type\"] == \"grpo_dynamic\":\n        grouped_rewards = (\n )\n\n    elif kwargs[\"adv_type\"] == \"reinpp\":\n        kwargs.update({\"rewards\": rewards.unsqueeze(0)})\n\n    elif kwargs[\"adv_type\"] == \"raw\":\n        kwargs.update({\"rewards\": advantages, returns\n\n\ndef preprocess_loss_inputs(\n    logprobs: torch.Tensor,\n    old_logprobs: torch.Tensor,\n    advantages: torch.Tensor,\n    logprob_type: Optional[str] = None,\n    single_action_dim: Optional[int] = None,\n    loss_mask: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,\n    loss_mask_sum: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,\n    values: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,\n    prev_values: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,\n    returns: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,\n    reward_type: Optional[str] = None,\n    versions: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,\n    **kwargs,\n) -> dict:\n    if reward_type == \"chunk_level\":\n        advantages = advantages.flatten()\n        if loss_mask is not None:\n            loss_mask = loss_mask.flatten()\n        if loss_mask_sum is not None:\n            loss_mask_sum = loss_mask_sum.flatten()\n        if values is not None:\n            values = values.flatten()\n        if prev_values is not None:\n            prev_values = prev_values.flatten()\n        if returns is not None:\n            returns = returns.flatten()\n\n    bsz = logprobs.shape[0]\n    proximal_logprobs = kwargs.get(\"proximal_logprobs\", None)\n    if logprob_type == \"token_level\":\n        # logprobs, old_logprobs: [bsz, num_action_chunks, action_dim] -> [bsz, num_action_chunks, action_dim]\n        logprobs = logprobs.reshape(bsz, -1, single_action_dim)\n        old_logprobs = old_logprobs.reshape(bsz, -1, single_action_dim)\n        if proximal_logprobs is not None:\n            proximal_logprobs = proximal_logprobs.reshape(bsz, -1, single_action_dim)\n        if versions is not None:\n            versions = versions.reshape(bsz, -1, single_action_dim)\n        if kwargs.get(\"loss_type\") == \"opd\":\n            assert advantages.shape == logprobs.shape, (\n                f\"OPD advantages shape {advantages.shape} must match \"\n                f\"logprobs shape {logprobs.shape}.\"\n            )\n        else:\n            advantages = advantages.unsqueeze(-1)\n        if loss_mask is not None:\n            loss_mask = loss_mask.unsqueeze(-1)\n        if loss_mask_sum is not None:\n            loss_mask_sum = loss_mask_sum.unsqueeze(-1)\n\n    elif logprob_type == \"action_level\":\n        # logprobs, old_logprobs: [bsz, num_action_chunks, action_dim] -> [bsz, num_action_chunks]\n        logprobs = logprobs.reshape(bsz, -1, single_action_dim).sum(dim=-1)\n        old_logprobs = old_logprobs.reshape(bsz, -1, single_action_dim).sum(dim=-1)\n        if proximal_logprobs is not None:\n            proximal_logprobs = proximal_logprobs.reshape(\n                bsz, -1, single_action_dim\n            ).sum(dim=-1)\n        if versions is not None:\n            versions = versions.reshape(bsz, -1, single_action_dim)[..., 0]\n\n    elif logprob_type == \"chunk_level\":\n        # logprobs, old_logprobs: [bsz, num_action_chunks, action_dim] -> [bsz]\n        logprobs = logprobs.reshape(bsz, -1, single_action_dim).sum(dim=[1, 2])\n        old_logprobs = old_logprobs.reshape(bsz, -1, single_action_dim).sum(dim=[1, 2])\n        if proximal_logprobs is not None:\n            proximal_logprobs = proximal_logprobs.reshape(\n                bsz, -1, single_action_dim\n            ).sum(dim=[1, 2])\n        if versions is not None:\n            versions = versions.reshape(bsz, -1, single_action_dim)[:, 0, 0]\n\n    target_shape = logprobs.shape\n    advantages = expand_to_target_dim(advantages, target_shape)\n    loss_mask = expand_to_target_dim(loss_mask, target_shape)\n    loss_mask_sum = expand_to_target_dim(loss_mask_sum, target_shape)\n    values = expand_to_target_dim(values, target_shape)\n    prev_values = expand_to_target_dim(prev_values, target_shape)\n    returns = expand_to_target_dim(returns, target_shape)\n    versions = expand_to_target_dim(versions, target_shape)\n\n    kwargs.update(\n        {\n            \"logprobs\": logprobs,\n            \"old_logprobs\": old_logprobs,\n            \"proximal_logprobs\": proximal_logprobs,\n            \"versions\": versions,\n            \"advantages\": advantages,\n            \"loss_mask\": loss_mask,\n            \"loss_mask_sum\": loss_mask_sum,\n            \"values\": values,\n            \"prev_values\": prev_values,\n            \"returns\": returns,\n        }\n    )\n\n    return kwargs\n\n\ndef postprocess_loss_metric(metrics_data: dict) -> dict:\n    for k, v in metrics_data.items():\n        if isinstance(v, torch.Tensor):\n            metrics_data[k] = v.detach().item()\n        elif isinstance(v, (float, int)):\n            metrics_data[k] = v\n    return metrics_data\n\n\ndef expand_to_target_dim(tensor, target_shape):\n    if tensor is None:\n        return None\n    if tensor.shape != target_shape:\n        while len(tensor.shape) < len(target_shape):\n            tensor = tensor.unsqueeze(-1)\n    return tensor\n\n\ndef safe_normalize(array, loss_mask):\n    valid_array = array[loss_mask]\n    if len(valid_array) > 0:\n        mean = valid_array.mean()\n        std = valid_array.std()\n        array = (array - mean) / (std + 1e-5)\n\n    return array\n"}

def _process_received_rollout_batch(\n        self, rollout_batch: dict[str, torch.Tensor]\n    ) -> dict[str, torch.Tensor]:\n        \"\"\"\n        original shape: [rollout_epoch x n_chunk_steps, rollout_batch = process_nested_dict_for_adv(rollout_batch, rollout_epoch)\n\n        if (\n            not self.cfg.env.train.auto_reset\n            and not self.cfg.env.train.ignore_terminations\n        ):\n            dones = rollout_batch[\n                \"dones\"\n            ]  # [n_chunk_step, rollout_epoch x bsz, num_action_chunks]\n            loss_mask, loss_mask_sum = loss_mask_sum[..., -1:]\n\n            rollout_batch[\"loss_mask\"] = loss_mask\n            rollout_batch[\"loss_mask_sum\"] = loss_mask_sum\n\n        # filter data by rewards\n        if self.cfg.algorithm.get(\"filter_rewards\", False):\n            rewards = rollout_batch[\n                \"rewards\"\n            ]  # [n_chunk_step, batch, num_action_chunks]\n            if      group_size = self.cfg.algorithm.group_size\n            assert batch_size % group_size == 0, (\n                f\"batch {batch_size} not divisible by group_size {group_size}\"\n            )\n            n_prompts = batch_size // group_size\n\n            # calculate rewards by prompt\n            rewards = rewards.transpose(\n                0, 1\n            )  # [batch, n_chunk_step, num_action_chunks]\n            rewards = rewards.reshape(rewards.shape[0], -1)  # [batch, n_step]\n            reward_matrix = rewards.reshape(\n                n_prompts, group_size, rewards.shape[-1]\n            )  # [n_prompts, group_size, n_step]\n            reward_matrix = reward_matrix.sum(dim=-1)  # [n_prompts, group_size]\n            mean_reward_in_group = reward_matrix.mean(dim=1)  # [n_prompts]\n\n            # mask\n            reward_filter_mask = (\n                mean_reward_in_group >= self.cfg.algorithm.rewards_lower_bound\n            ) & (\n                mean_reward_in_group <= self.cfg.algorithm.rewards_upper_bound\n            )  # [n_prompts]\n\n            # extend mask dimension\n            reward_filter_mask = reward_filter_mask.repeat_interleave(\n                group_size\n            )  # [batch]\n            reward_filter_mask = (\n                reward_filter_mask.unsqueeze(0).expand(n_chunk_step, -1).unsqueeze(-1)\n            )  # [n_chunk_step, batch, 1]\n\n            # update loss_mask\n            if rollout_batch.get(\"loss_mask\", None) is not None:\n                rollout_batch[\"loss_mask\"] = (\n                    reward_filter_mask & rollout_batch[\"loss_mask\"]\n                )\n            else:\n                rollout_batch[\"loss_mask\"] =        return rollout_metrics\n\n    @Worker.timer(\"actor/compute_opd_teacher_logprobs\")\n    def compute_opd_teacher_logprobs(self) -> None:\n        assert self.rollout_batch.get(\"teacher_logprobs\", None) is None, (\n            \"OPD teacher_logprobs must be computed after rollout on actor workers.\"\n        )\n        assert          compute_values=False,\n                        use_cache=False,\n                        **kwargs,\n                    )\n                teacher_logprobs.append(teacher_output[\"logprobs\"].detach().cpu())\n\n        teacher_logprobs = torch.cat(teacher_logprobs, dim=0)\n        expected_shape = (flat_batch_size, *prev_logprobs.shape[2:])\n        assert teacher_logprobs.shape == expected_shape, (\n            f\"teacher_logprobs shape {teacher_logprobs.shape} must match \"\n            f\"flattened student logprobs shape {expected_shape}.\"\n        )\n        self.rollout_batch[\"teacher_logprobs\"] = teacher_logprobs.reshape(\n            time_dim, batch_dim, *teacher_logprobs.shape[1:]\n        )\n\n        teacher_model.to(\"cpu\")\n        clear_memory()\n\n    def _get_opd_teacher_model(self):\n        if self._opd_teacher_model is None:\n            teacher_model_config = build_expert_model_config(\n                self.cfg, self.cfg.actor.model\n            )\n            teacher_model = get_model(teacher_model_config)\n            if           teacher_model.load_state_dict(teacher_model_dict)\n            teacher_model.eval()\n            teacher_model.requires_grad_(False)\n            teacher_model.to(\"cpu\")\n            self._opd_teacher_model = teacher_model\n        return self._opd_teacher_model\n\n    def _build_sft_data_loader(self):\n        if SupportedModel(self.cfg.actor.model.model_type) in [SupportedModel.OPENPI]:\n            repo_id = resolve_lerobot_repo_id(self.cfg.actor.get(\"sft_data_path\"))\n            if repo_id is None:\n                raise ValueError(\n                    \"actor.sft_data_path must be set to a local dataset path or \"\n                    \"LeRobot repo id when enable_sft_co_train=True.\"\n                )\n\n            import raise ValueError(\n                    \"config_name is required when enable_sft_co_train=True\"\n                )\n            training_config_name = self.cfg.actor.config_name\n data_kwargs=getattr(self.cfg.actor.model, \"openpi_data\", None),\n            )\n            self.data_loader = _data.create_data_loader(\n                data_loader_config, framework=\"pytorch\",        else:\n            raise KeyError(\n                f\"not support such model type {self.cfg.actor.model.model_type} for SFT right now.\"\n            )\n\n    def _train_sft_epoch(\n        self, metrics_data: dict[str, torch.Tensor], loss: torch.Tensor\n    ) -> torch.Tensor:\n        \"\"\"\n        Train one epoch of SFT.\n        \"\"\"\n        metrics_data[\"ppo_loss\"] = loss.clone().detach().item()\n\n        # Get next data batch\n        try:\n            observation, actions = next(self.sft_iterator)\n        except StopIteration:\n            self.train_epoch += 1\n            self.data_loader.set_epoch(self.train_epoch)\n            self.sft_iterator = iter(self.data_loader)\n            observation, actions = next(self.sft_iterator)\n\n        sft_loss = self.model(\n            data=(observation, actions),\n            forward_type=ForwardType.SFT,\n        )\n        metrics_data[\"sft_loss\"] = sft_loss.detach().item()\n        total_loss = loss + self.sft_loss_weight * sft_loss\n        loss = total_loss\n\n        metrics_data[\"loss_ratio\"] = (\n            np.abs(metrics_data[\"sft_loss\"]) / np.abs(metrics_data[\"ppo_loss\"])\n            if np.abs(metrics_data[\"ppo_loss\"]) > 0\n            else float(\"inf\")\n        )\n        if metrics_data[\"loss_ratio\"] > 1e5:\n            self.logger.warning(\n                \"SFT/PPO loss imbalance detected: \"\n                f\"ratio={metrics_data['loss_ratio']:.3e}, \"\n                f\"sft_loss={metrics_data['sft_loss']:.6f}, \"\n                f\"ppo_loss={metrics_data['ppo_loss']:.6f}, \"\n                f\"sft_loss_weight={self.sft_loss_weight:.6f}\"\n            )\n        return loss\n\n    def _prepare_dvac_train_step(self) -> None:\n        forward_inputs = self.rollout_batch.get(\"forward_inputs\")\n        if not isinstance(forward_inputs, dict):\n            raise ValueError(\"DVAC gradient weighting requires forward_inputs.\")\n        variance_key = f\"dvac_v_l{self.dvac_selected_l}\"\n        if variance_key not in forward_inputs:\n            raise ValueError(f\"Missing train-time DVAC tensor: {variance_key}\")\n        variance = forward_inputs.pop(variance_key)\n\n        local_stats = local_log_v_sufficient_statistics(\n            variance, log_eps=self.dvac_recent_stats.log_eps\n        ).to(self.device)\n        torch.distributed.all_reduce(local_stats, op=torch.distributed.ReduceOp.SUM)\n        count, value_sum, value_sq_sum = local_stats.detach().cpu().tolist()\n        current_stats = DVACStepStats(\n            runner_step=int(self.version),\n            count=int(round(count)),\n            value_sum=float(value_sum),\n            value_sq_sum=float(value_sq_sum),\n        )\n\n        weights, clipped_z, warmup, history = self.dvac_recent_stats.compute_weights(\n            variance\n        )\n        forward_inputs[\"dvac_weights\"] = weights\n        self._dvac_pending_step = {\n            \"runner_step\": int(self.version),\n            \"current_stats\": current_stats,\n            \"history\": history,\n            \"warmup\": warmup,\n            \"variance\": variance.detach().cpu().contiguous(),\n            \"weights\": weights.detach().cpu().contiguous(),\n            \"clipped_z\": clipped_z.detach().cpu().contiguous(),\n            \"advantages\": self.rollout_batch[\"advantages\"].detach().cpu().contiguous(),\n            \"rewards\": self.rollout_batch[\"rewards\"].detach().cpu().contiguous(),\n            \"loss_mask\": (\n                None\n                if self.rollout_batch.get(\"loss_mask\") is None\n                else self.rollout_batch[\"loss_mask\"].detach().cpu().contiguous()\n            ),\n            \"metrics\": {\n                \"actor/dvac_warmup\": float(warmup),\n                \"actor/dvac_history_mean\": float(history[\"history_mean\"]),\n                \"actor/dvac_history_std\": float(history[\"history_std\"]),\n                \"actor/dvac_current_mean\": current_stats.mean,\n                \"actor/dvac_current_std\": current_stats.std,\n                \"actor/dvac_weight_mean\": float(weights.float().mean().item()),\n                \"actor/dvac_weight_sq_mean\": float(\n                    weights.float().square().mean().item()\n                ),\n                \"actor/dvac_z_low_clip_fraction\": float(\n                    (clipped_z <= -self.dvac_recent_stats.z_clip).float().mean().item()\n                ),\n                \"actor/dvac_z_high_clip_fraction\": float(\n                    (clipped_z >= self.dvac_recent_stats.z_clip).float().mean().item()\n                ),\n            },\n        }\n\n    def _write_dvac_step_artifact(self, pending: dict) -> None:\n        if not bool(self.dvac_train_cfg.get(\"save_step_tensors\", True)):\n            return\n        path = self.dvac_output_dir / (\n            f\"runner_step_{int(pending['runner_step']):04d}.pt\"\n        )\n        if path.exists():\n            raise FileExistsError(f\"DVAC step artifact already exists: {path}\")\n        temporary = path.with_suffix(\".partial.pt\")\n        torch.save(\n            {\n                \"schema_version\": 1,\n                \"runner_step\": int(pending[\"runner_step\"]),\n                \"actor_rank\": int(self._rank),\n                \"application\": \"action_advantage\"\n                    else None\n                ),\n                \"selected_l\": int(self.dvac_selected_l),\n                \"warmup\": bool(pending[\"warmup\"]),\n                \"history\": dict(pending[\"history\"]),\n                \"current_stats\": {\n                    \"count\": pending[\"current_stats\"].count,\n                    \"mean\": pending[\"current_stats\"].mean,\n                    \"std\": pending[\"current_stats\"].std,\n                },\n                \"variance\": pending[\"variance\"],\n                \"weights\": pending[\"weights\"],\n                \"clipped_z\": pending[\"clipped_z\"],\n                \"advantages\": pending[\"advantages\"],\n                \"rewards\": pending[\"rewards\"],\n                \"loss_mask\": pending[\"loss_mask\"],\n            },\n            temporary,\n        )\n        os.replace(temporary, path)\n\n    @Worker.timer(\"run_training\")\n    def run_training(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Run the training process using the received rollout batch.\n        \"\"\"\n        if self.is_weight_offloaded:\n            self.load_param_and_grad(self.device)\n        if self.is_optimizer_offloaded:\n            self.load_optimizer(self.device)\n\n        if self.cfg.algorithm.loss_type == \"opd\":\n            target_steps = int(self.rollout_batch[\"advantages\"].shape[0])\n            for key in [\n                \"prev_logprobs\",\n                \"forward_inputs\",\n                \"loss_mask\",\n                \"loss_mask_sum\",\n            ]:\n                assert key in self.rollout_batch, f\"OPD training requires {key}.\"\n                self.rollout_batch[key] = trim_nested_tensor_time_dim(\n                    self.rollout_batch[key], target_steps, (key,)\n                )\n\n        self.model.train()\n        if self.dvac_train_enabled:\n            self._prepare_dvac_train_step()\n g.manual_seed(self.cfg.actor.seed + self._rank)\n        shuffle_id = torch.randperm(rollout_size, generator=g)\n\n        with torch.no_grad():\n            self.rollout_batch = process_nested_dict_for_train(\n                self.rollout_batch, shuffle_id\n            )\n\n        # Split to make minibatch iterator for updating the actor\n        # See PPO paper for details. assert rollout_size % batch_size_per_rank == 0, (\n            f\"{rollout_size} is not divisible by {batch_size_per_rank}\"\n        )\n        metrics = {}\n        update_epoch = rollout_size // batch_size_per_rank,\n            )\n            for train_global_batch in rollout_dataloader_iter:\n                # split batch into micro_batches\n self.cfg.actor.global_batch_size\n                    // torch.distributed.get_world_size()\n                )\n                assert train_global_batch_size % self.cfg.actor.micro_batch_size == 0, train_global_batch,\n                    train_global_batch_size // self.cfg.actor.micro_batch_size,\n                )\n\n                self.optimizer.zero_grad()\n                for idx, batch in enumerate(train_micro_batch):\n                    self.train_micro_batch(\n                        micro_batch=batch,\n                        metrics=metrics,\n                        is_last=(idx + 1) == self.gradient_accumulation,\n                    )\n                    # avoid gpu memory leak\n                    train_micro_batch[idx] = None\n                    del batch\n\n                self.torch_platform.empty_cache()\n\n                grad_norm, lr_list = self.optimizer_step()\n                data = {\n                    \"actor/grad_norm\": grad_norm,\n                    \"actor/lr\": lr_list[0],\n                }\n                if len(lr_list) > 1:\n                    data[\"critic/lr\"] = lr_list[1]\n                append_to_dict(metrics, data)\n        if self.dvac_train_enabled:\n            append_to_dict(metrics, self._dvac_pending_step[\"metrics\"])\n\n        # put LR scheduler step here\n        self.lr_scheduler.step()\n        self.optimizer.zero_grad()\n        clear_memory()\n        explained_variance_stats = pop_critic_explained_variance_stats(metrics)\n        mean_metric_dict = {key: np.mean(value) for key, value in metrics.items()}\n        mean_metric_dict = all_reduce_dict(\n            mean_metric_dict, op=torch.distributed.ReduceOp.AVG\n        )\n        if self.dvac_train_enabled:\n            weight_mean            self._write_dvac_step_artifact(pending)\n            self.dvac_recent_stats.push(pending[\"current_stats\"])\n            self._dvac_pending_step = None\n        if mean_metric_dict[CRITIC_EXPLAINED_VARIANCE_KEY] = (\n                compute_critic_explained_variance_from_stats(reduced_stats).item()\n            )\n\n        return mean_metric_dict\n\n    def train_micro_batch(\n        self,\n        micro_batch: dict[str, torch.Tensor],\n        metrics: dict[str, list[float]],\n        *,\n        is_last: bool,\n    ) -> None:\n        micro_batch = put_tensor_device(micro_batch, self.device)\n        backward_ctx = self.before_micro_batch(self.model, is_last_micro_batch=is_last)\n        advantages = micro_batch[\"advantages\"]\n         for key, value in forward_inputs.items()\n                if key != \"dvac_weights\"\n            }\n\n        kwargs = {}\n        if SupportedModel(self.cfg.actor.model.model_type) in [\n            SupportedModel.OPENVLA,\n            SupportedModel.OPENVLA_OFT,\n        ]:\n            kwargs[\"temperature\"] = self.cfg.rollout.sampling_params.temperature_train\n            kwargs[\"top_k\"] = self.cfg.rollout.sampling_params.top_k\n        elif SupportedModel(self.cfg.actor.model.model_type) in [\n            SupportedModel.GR00T,\n            SupportedModel.GR00T_N1D6,\n            SupportedModel.GR00T_N1D7,\n            SupportedModel.ABOT_M0,\n        ]:\n            kwargs[\"prev_logprobs\"] = prev_logprobs\n\n        compute_values = self.cfg.algorithm.adv_type == \"gae\"\n        with self.amp_context:\n            output_dict = self.model(\n                forward_inputs=model_forward_inputs,\n                compute_logprobs=True,\n                compute_entropy=self.cfg.algorithm.entropy_bonus > 0,\n                compute_values=compute_values,\n                use_cache=False,\n                **kwargs,\n            )\n\n        if SupportedModel(self.cfg.actor.model.model_type) in [\n            SupportedModel.GR00T,\n            SupportedModel.GR00T_N1D6,\n SupportedModel.GR00T_N1D6,\n            SupportedModel.GR00T_N1D7,\n        ]:\n            loss_kwargs[\"clip_ratio_c\"] = self.cfg.algorithm.get(\"clip_ratio_c\", 3.0)\n            if _dvac_sidecar_path(self, checkpoint_path: str) -> Path:\n        return Path(checkpoint_path) / f\"dvac_state_rank{self._rank:04d}.json\"\n\n    def save_checkpoint(self, save_path: str, step: int = 0) -> None:\n        super().save_checkpoint(save_path, step)\n        if not self.dvac_train_enabled:\n            return\n        if self._dvac_pending_step is not None:\n            raise \"actor_world_size\": int(self._world_size),\n            \"mode\": self.dvac_train_mode,\n            \"application\": self.dvac_train_application,\n            \"selected_l\": \"\\n\",\n            encoding=\"utf-8\",\n        )\n        os.replace(temporary, path)\n\n    def load_checkpoint(self, load_path: str) -> None:\n        restored_stats = None\n        if self.dvac_train_enabled:\n            path = self._dvac_sidecar_path(load_path)\n            if not path.is_file():\n                raise FileNotFoundError(\n                    f\"Exact DVAC resume     \"actor_rank\": int(self._rank),\n                \"actor_world_size\": int(self._world_size),\n                \"mode\": self.dvac_train_mode,\n                \"application\": self.dvac_train_application,\n                \"selected_l\": int(self.dvac_selected_l),\n            }\n            for key, value in expected.items():\n                checkpoint_value = payload.get(key)\n                if key == \"application\" and checkpoint_value is None:\n                    checkpoint_value = \"logprob_st\"\n                if checkpoint_value != value:\n                    raise ValueError(\n                        f\"DVAC resume mismatch for {key}: \"\n                        f\"checkpoint={checkpoint_value!r}, current={value!r}\"\n                    )\n            restored_stats = self._new_dvac_recent_stats()\n            restored_stats.load_state_dict(payload[\"recent_stats\"])\n\n        super().load_checkpoint(load_path)\n        if restored_stats is not None:\n            self.dvac_recent_stats = restored_stats\n\n    def set_global_step(self, global_step: int) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Set the global step for the model, if needed.\n        \"\"\"\n        self.version = global_step\n        if hasattr(self.model, \"set_global_step\"):\n            self.model.set_global_step(global_step)\n\n    def finish_global_batch(self, metrics: dict[str, list[float]]) -> None:\n        self.torch_platform.empty_cache()\n        grad_norm, lr_list = self.optimizer_step()\n        self.optimizer.zero_grad()\n        metric_data = {\n            \"actor/grad_norm\": grad_norm,\n            \"actor/lr\": lr_list[0],\n        }\n        if len(lr_list) > 1:\n            metric_data[\"critic/lr\"] = lr_list[1]\n        append_to_dict(metrics, metric_data)\n"}

self.rollout_queue_size = self.cfg.rollout.get(\"rollout_queue_size\", 0)\n\n    def init_worker(self):\n        rollout_model_config = copy.deepcopy(self.model_cfg)\n        with             resolved_config=OmegaConf.to_yaml(self.cfg, resolve=True),\n            )\n\n        if self.dvac_train_enabled:\n            if self.rlt_feature_model is not None or self.expert_model is     raise ValueError(\n                    \"DVAC gradient weighting does not support: \"\n                    + \", \".join(active_modes)\n                )\n\n        if self.hf_model.enable_torch_compile(mode=mode)\n        if self.enable_cuda_graph and not self.enable_offload:\n            self.hf_model.capture_cuda_graph(\n
